      (YulangClosure.PaperTypes.shiftTermSubstitution rho)

YULANG_AUDIT_BEGIN YulangClosure.PaperTypes.unsupported_raw_falsifier
YulangClosure.PaperTypes.unsupported_raw_falsifier : ∀ (rho : Nat → YulangClosure.PaperTypes.Value) (n : Nat)
  (ts : List YulangClosure.PaperTypes.Ty) (vs : List YulangClosure.PaperTypes.Value)
  (handler : YulangClosure.PaperTypes.HandlerPlan) (c : YulangClosure.PaperTypes.Computation),
  (YulangClosure.PaperTypes.termSubstituteValue rho (YulangClosure.PaperTypes.Value.variant n ts vs)).isSome =
      Bool.false ∧
    (YulangClosure.PaperTypes.termSubstituteValue rho (YulangClosure.PaperTypes.Value.nominal n ts vs)).isSome =
        Bool.false ∧
      (YulangClosure.PaperTypes.termSubstituteComputation rho
              (YulangClosure.PaperTypes.Computation.recursive [] c)).isSome =
          Bool.false ∧
        (YulangClosure.PaperTypes.termSubstituteComputation rho (c.handle handler)).isSome = Bool.false
'YulangClosure.PaperTypes.unsupported_raw_falsifier' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
YULANG_AUDIT_END YulangClosure.PaperTypes.unsupported_raw_falsifier

        (YulangClosure.PaperTypes.Value.lambda YulangClosure.PaperTypes.EntryMode.value profile 7

          (YulangClosure.PaperTypes.graphFormalReplacements formals rho)

@YulangClosure.PaperTypes.PaperValueTyped.substituteFreshGraphFormals : ∀
  {d : YulangClosure.PaperTypes.DeclarationTable} {old new : YulangClosure.PaperTypes.GraphEnvironment}
  {source target : YulangClosure.PaperTypes.TypeEnvironment} {v : YulangClosure.PaperTypes.Value}
  {a : YulangClosure.PaperTypes.Ty} {rho : Nat → YulangClosure.PaperTypes.Value}
  (formals : List (Nat × YulangClosure.PaperTypes.Ty)),
  YulangClosure.PaperTypes.GraphLookupPreserved old new →
    (∀ (identity : Nat) (a : YulangClosure.PaperTypes.Ty),
        (identity, a) ∈ formals → YulangClosure.PaperTypes.lookupGraphEnvironment new identity = Option.some a) →
      YulangClosure.PaperTypes.TermSubstitutionTyped d old source target rho →
        YulangClosure.PaperTypes.PaperValueTyped d old (List.map Prod.snd formals ++ source) v a →
          ∃ result,
            YulangClosure.PaperTypes.termSubstituteValue (YulangClosure.PaperTypes.graphFormalReplacements formals rho)
                  v =
                Option.some result ∧
              YulangClosure.PaperTypes.PaperValueTyped d new target result a
